99 & \xrightarrow{\phantom{(4 \times 2) + 5}} 27 \xrightarrow{\phantom{(4 \times 2) + 5}} 11 \xrightarrow{\phantom{(4 \times 2) + 5}} 3 && (3 \text{ steps})
